- Sat 03 Oct 04:48 AEST Item 9 BUG-358 built: draft #558 (head 6b5d7f2d; failing tests e0c83663, 4 red), base rel-20261008u 3d83cde1. Cause read from code + echoes: ERASE alone is one U (driver run 1), so the empty U2 is FREGENALL's own: CE's undo mark (UNDO Begin/End) opened INSIDE the command split its one Undo step into 'FREGENALL GROUP' + an empty step. Fix: CommandExecutionService holds the command as one step (layer restore included) when CMDACTIVE bit 1 is set; UndoMarkScope opens no mark inside it; outside a command the mark opens as before. Reaches every Structural command run through it (footing commands, CSLOADPATH, quick PFC/SF/THC, legends, infill, plan levels); U echo becomes 'FREGENALL' not 'FREGENALL GROUP'. BUILT net48 0 errors; TESTED net10 focused 232/232; not full suite; live logs untouched. Opus review required (Undo path); driver check on DRV2_S1 in the PR. NOT tested in AutoCAD, not installed. usage: not readable headless.

ITEM 2 (MOVE): FoundationService.OnDriverEntitiesPossiblyModified (FoundationService.cs ~479-489) calls IsRegisteredDriverHandle || IsRegisteredRaftHostHandle per moved line; each goes GetDrawingKey -> HasOpenDocument (FoundationRegistryService.cs ~428-443), a walk of every open drawing; a footing line pays 1 walk, any other moved object 2; not cached across the loop. ITEM 4 (FADOPT): CollectTextCandidates (FoundationService.cs ~3887) walks model space once; text on other layers runs TryResolveFootingEntity (linked text pays one HasOpenDocument walk each); TryFindBestLabelFor (~3941) is footings x label candidates x sort. DECIDE: fix only if MOVE ALL adds over 250 ms per 1 000 lines with 5 drawings open, or FADOPT over 1 s at 20 000 texts; the cheap fix for item 2 is one HasOpenDocument per batch (cache the drawing key across the loop); for item 4, collect label candidates once with their positions and use a nearest-first search instead of a sort per footing. ANSWER: STR, BWT and CSREOBAR are on NO menu and NO button of the CE STRUC tab. STR = typed alias of CE_STIRRUP (Stirrup) and BWT = typed alias of CE_BARWEIGHT (Bar Weight): both registry entries are hidden:true ('ST6-14: hidden from the ribbon and menus, not retired; typed STR/STIRRUP and BWT/BARWEIGHT still work'; Bar Weight's job moves to CSBARSCHEDULE). CSREOBAR (label 'Reo bar', group Footings/Rebar) is hidden:true too, note 'Hidden until Codex passes it (card S4 section 4 item 4)'. Had they been visible they would sit in Rebar & Bracing > 'Reinforcement'. So the driver must TYPE them. SOURCE: the shipping tab is built at run time by Modules/Structural/Services/RibbonCommandHubService.cs (tab 'CE STRUC', id CE_STRUC_SUITE_COMMANDS_TAB) from Assets/Config/command-registry.json plus the commands found in the DLL; CeStrucRibbonContributor.cs ('CE Structural') is compile-removed (future unified ribbon); the CE_STRUC_SUITE_MENU.cuix has old toolbars and an empty ribbon, not this tab. METHOD: I reproduced the panel/menu rules in a script over the registry (merge of code-found names, duplicate/alias hiding, large picks, one-item menus shown as a plain button) with DEFAULT settings (one name per tool OFF, Beam/Tag drop-downs OFF, 'split long lists' OFF, so no menu is split: the three long Annotate menus are Beam & Support Tags 56, Hatching 43, Legacy Drafting 27 items: that is why Codex could not read them on one screen).
